# Luminary Kids — hands-on lesson plans

Generated by `node content/build.js` from `content/lessons/*.json` — edit the lessons there (or in the admin panel), not here.
Every line a child hears has an audio file name; record it with that name (see the recording scripts in `dist/`).

## Grade 1 · Lesson 1 — የአትክልት ቦታ ቆጠራ (Counting in the garden)

Place on the map: garden · Mini-game: coffee · Hook video: `video/count10.mp4` (31.3 s) · Manim code: `animations/lessons/count10.py`

```

### 1. Hook video script

|---|---|---|---|
| 0.0 s | የትኛው ይበልጣል? | Which is greater? | `lesson.g2_compare.title` |
| 3.7 s | በገበያ ሁለት ነጋዴዎች እንቁላል ይሸጣሉ። አንዱ አርባ ሁለት፣ ሌላው ሃያ አራት እንቁላሎች አሉት። | Two sellers at the market have eggs. One has forty-two, the other twenty-four. | `lesson.g2_compare.story` |

### 4. Play — market

ገበያ እንሂድ! ያለንን ብር በሙሉ የሚጨርሱ ዕቃዎችን እንግዛ።<br><small>Let's go to the market! Buy things that use up exactly all our birr.</small> · `ex.g2_birr.19.mp3`

[{"budget":30,"goods":[{"pic":"bread","am":"ዳቦ","en":"Bread","price":12},{"pic":"banana","am":"ሙዝ","en":"Bananas","price":18},{"pic":"egg","am":"እንቁላል","en":"Eggs","price":25}]},{"budget":50,"goods":[{"pic":"pen","am":"እስክሪብቶ","en":"Pen","price":15},{"pic":"book","am":"ደብተር","en":"Exercise book","price":20},{"pic":"banana","am":"ሙዝ","en":"Bananas","price":15}]},{"budget":100,"goods":[{"pic":"shoes","am":"ጫማ","en":"Shoes","price":65},{"pic":"bag","am":"ቦርሳ","en":"Bag","price":80},{"pic":"book","am":"ደብተር","en":"Exercise book","price":35}]}]
```
